4	//   PlaceSingle = 8-10 个小山丘(二次贝塞尔 PlaceCurvedLine,曲线以下到 Surface+√x*3 全转沙 53,
5	//   曲线上方 10 格清非沙物) + 1-2 个 0.8 scale 中央大山丘。
6	// 同 pass 还掷金字塔候选(cs:11591-11599:80% 概率,origin±200 列地表+20 → GenVars.PyrX/PyrY,
7	// 这里写 gs.pyramidSpots,由 StructuresPass.runPyramidPass 按 cs:15426 段消费)。
8	// 数量:Dunes.Count = WorldGenRange 1-2 × WorldWidth/4200(Configuration.json)。
9	import type { TileStore } from '../../TileStore';
10	import type { RNG } from '../../../core/rng';
11	import type { GenState } from './GenState';
12	import { TILE_BY_KEY } from '../../../data/tiles';
13	
14	const SAND = TILE_BY_KEY['sand']!;
15	const CLOUD = TILE_BY_KEY['v_189_cloud_block']!;
16	const RAIN_CLOUD = TILE_BY_KEY['v_196_rain_cloud_block']!;
17	
18	interface DunesArea { left: number; top: number; width: number; height: number }
19	interface DunesDesc {
20	  area: DunesArea;
21	  surface: Int16Array; // SurfaceMap 高度(相对 area.left-20)
22	  surfaceX: number;    // SurfaceMap 起始列(= area.left-20)
23	  windRight: boolean;  // WindDirection
24	}
25	
26	const HEIGHT_SCALE = 1.0; // Configuration.json DunesBiome.HeightScale
27	
28	/** SurfaceMap.FromArea(DunesBiome 描述用):每列自 y=50 向下扫 maxTilesY/2 格,
29	 *  跳过云族(Clouds)取首个实心;无则 maxTilesY/2+50(SurfaceMap.cs:41-64) */
30	function surfaceMap(st: TileStore, startX: number, width: number): Int16Array {
31	  const limit = Math.floor(st.h / 2);
32	  const out = new Int16Array(width);
33	  for (let i = 0; i < width; i++) {
34	    const x = startX + i;
35	    let found = false;
36	    let y = 0;
37	    for (let j = 50; j < 50 + limit; j++) {
38	      if (!st.inBounds(x, j) || !st.flags[st.idx(x, j)]) continue;
39	      const t = st.type[st.idx(x, j)];
40	      if (t === CLOUD || t === RAIN_CLOUD) { found = false; continue; }
41	      if (!found) { y = j; found = true; }
42	    }
43	    out[i] = found ? y : limit + 50;
44	  }
45	  return out;
46	}
47	
48	export function runDunesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
49	  const { w, h } = st;
50	  // WorldGenRange(1,2,ScaleWith:WorldWidth).GetRandom:ScaleValue=(int)(w/4200×v)
51	  const ws = w / 4200;
52	  const lo = Math.max(1, Math.trunc(ws * 1));
53	  const hi = Math.max(lo, Math.trunc(ws * 2));
54	  const count = rng.int(lo, hi);
55	  const pyramidChance = 0.8; // Configuration.json Dunes.ChanceOfPyramid
56	  const scale = w / 4200;    // cs:11562 num2
57	  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
58	    // RandomWorldPoint(0, 500, 0, 500)(cs:11570)+ 拒绝带(cs:11572-11588):
59	    // 丛林 ±600·num2 / 中央 ±300 / 雪原带 ±300;超 w 次放松丛林、超 2w 次放松雪原
60	    let ox = 0, oy = 0, tries = 0;
61	    for (;;) {
62	      ox = rng.int(500, w - 501); // Next(500, w-500)
63	      oy = rng.int(0, h - 1);     // Next(0, maxTilesY)
64	      let fJungle = Math.abs(ox - gs.jungleX) < Math.trunc(600 * scale);
65	      const fCenter = Math.abs(ox - w / 2) < 300;
66	      let fSnow = ox > gs.snowOriginLeft - 300 && ox < gs.snowOriginRight + 300;
67	      tries++;
68	      if (tries >= w) fJungle = false;
69	      if (tries >= 2 * w) fSnow = false;
70	      if (!(fJungle || fCenter || fSnow)) break;
71	    }
72	    placeDunes(st, rng, ox, oy);
73	    // 金字塔候选(cs:11591-11599)
74	    if (rng.next() <= pyramidChance) {
75	      const px = rng.int(ox - 200, ox + 199); // Next(origin.X-200, origin.X+200)
76	      for (let j = 0; j < h; j++) {
77	        if (st.inBounds(px, j) && st.flags[st.idx(px, j)]) {
78	          gs.pyramidSpots.push({ x: px, y: j + 20 });
79	          break;
80	        }
81	      }
82	    }
83	  }
84	}
85	
86	/** DunesBiome.Place(cs:DunesBiome.cs:53-64):左右两个单侧沙丘 */
87	function placeDunes(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, ox: number, oy: number): void {
88	  const h1 = Math.trunc(rng.int(60, 99) * HEIGHT_SCALE); // Next(60,100)
89	  const h2 = Math.trunc(rng.int(60, 99) * HEIGHT_SCALE);
90	  const w1 = rng.int(150, 250); // SingleDunesWidth.GetRandom(含两端)
91	  const w2 = rng.int(150, 250);
92	  placeSingle(st, rng, makeDesc(st, rng, ox - Math.trunc(w1 / 2) + 30, oy, w1, h1));
93	  placeSingle(st, rng, makeDesc(st, rng, ox + Math.trunc(w2 / 2) - 30, oy, w2, h2));
94	}
95	
96	function makeDesc(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number, width: number, height: number): DunesDesc {
97	  // DunesDescription.CreateFromPlacement:area = origin-(w/2,h/2, w, h);
98	  // SurfaceMap.FromArea(area.Left-20, area.Width+40);风向后随机(DunesBiome.cs:26-36)
99	  const left = x - Math.trunc(width / 2);
100	  const area: DunesArea = { left, top: y - Math.trunc(height / 2), width, height };
101	  const sx = left - 20;
102	  return { area, surface: surfaceMap(st, sx, width + 40), surfaceX: sx, windRight: rng.int(0, 1) !== 0 };
103	}
104	
105	/** DunesBiome.PlaceSingle(DunesBiome.cs:66-87) */
106	function placeSingle(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, d: DunesDesc): void {
107	  const num = rng.int(0, 2) + 8; // Next(3)+8 → 8-10
108	  for (let i = 0; i < num - 1; i++) {
109	    const n2 = Math.trunc((2 / num) * d.area.width); // C# int
110	    let n3 = Math.trunc((i / num) * d.area.width + d.area.left) + Math.trunc((n2 * 2) / 5);
111	    n3 += rng.int(-5, 5);
112	    const n4 = i / (num - 2);
113	    const n5 = 1 - Math.abs(n4 - 0.5) * 2;
114	    placeHill(st, rng, d, n3 - Math.trunc(n2 / 2), n3 + Math.trunc(n2 / 2), (n5 * 0.3 + 0.2) * HEIGHT_SCALE);
115	  }
116	  const n6 = rng.int(0, 1) + 1; // Next(2)+1
117	  for (let j = 0; j < n6; j++) {
118	    const n7 = Math.trunc(d.area.width / 2);
119	    const cx = d.area.left + Math.trunc(d.area.width / 2) + rng.int(-10, 10);
120	    placeHill(st, rng, d, cx - Math.trunc(n7 / 2), cx + Math.trunc(n7 / 2), 0.8 * HEIGHT_SCALE);
121	  }
122	}
123	
124	/** DunesBiome.PlaceHill(DunesBiome.cs:89-108) */
125	function placeHill(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, d: DunesDesc, startX: number, endX: number, scale: number): void {
126	  // SurfaceMap 越界钳到边缘列(原版越界会 IndexOutOfRange,此处防御)
127	  const surfAt = (x: number) => {
128	    const i = Math.max(0, Math.min(d.surface.length - 1, x - d.surfaceX));
129	    return d.surface[i];
130	  };
131	  const startPoint = { x: startX, y: surfAt(startX) };
132	  const endPoint = { x: endX, y: surfAt(endX) };
133	  const center = { x: Math.trunc((startPoint.x + endPoint.x) / 2), y: Math.trunc((startPoint.y + endPoint.y) / 2) - Math.trunc(35 * scale) };
134	  const big = Math.trunc((endPoint.x - center.x) / 4);
135	  const small = Math.trunc((endPoint.x - center.x) / 16);
136	  if (!d.windRight) center.x -= rng.int(small, big);      // Next(min, num+1)
137	  else center.x += rng.int(small, big);
138	  const p2 = { x: 0, y: Math.trunc(scale * 12) };
139	  const p3 = { x: Math.trunc(p2.x / -2), y: Math.trunc(p2.y / -2) };
140	  placeCurvedLine(st, d, startPoint, center, d.windRight ? p3 : p2);
141	  placeCurvedLine(st, d, center, endPoint, !d.windRight ? p3 : p2);
142	}
143	
144	/** DunesBiome.PlaceCurvedLine(DunesBiome.cs:110-161):二次贝塞尔取样,
145	 *  曲线以上 10 格清非沙物、曲线到 Surface+√(num3)*3 全列转沙(ResetToType(53)) */
146	function placeCurvedLine(
147	  st: TileStore, d: DunesDesc,
148	  startPoint: { x: number; y: number }, endPoint: { x: number; y: number },
149	  anchorOffset: { x: number; y: number },
150	): void {
151	  const p = {
152	    x: Math.trunc((startPoint.x + endPoint.x) / 2) + anchorOffset.x,
153	    y: Math.trunc((startPoint.y + endPoint.y) / 2) + anchorOffset.y,
154	  };
155	  const step = 0.5 / (endPoint.x - startPoint.x);
156	  let prevX = -1, prevY = -1;
157	  let t = 0, guard = 0;
158	  while (t <= 1 && guard++ < 10000) {
159	    const ax = startPoint.x + (p.x - startPoint.x) * t;
160	    const ay = startPoint.y + (p.y - startPoint.y) * t;
161	    const bx = p.x + (endPoint.x - p.x) * t;
162	    const by = p.y + (endPoint.y - p.y) * t;
163	    const px = Math.trunc(ax + (bx - ax) * t); // Vector2D.Lerp(...).ToPoint() 截断
164	    const py = Math.trunc(ay + (by - ay) * t);
165	    t += step;
166	    if (px === prevX && py === prevY) continue;
167	    prevX = px; prevY = py;
168	    const centerX = d.area.left + Math.trunc(d.area.width / 2);
169	    const n3 = Math.trunc(d.area.width / 2) - Math.abs(px - centerX);
170	    const surf = d.surface[Math.max(0, Math.min(d.surface.length - 1, px - d.surfaceX))];
171	    const n4 = surf + Math.trunc(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, n3)) * 3);
172	    if (!st.inBounds(px, py)) continue;
173	    for (let i = py - 10; i < py; i++) {
174	      if (!st.inBounds(px, i)) continue;
175	      const ti = st.idx(px, i);
176	      if (st.flags[ti] && st.type[ti] !== SAND) {
177	        st.type[ti] = 0; st.flags[ti] = 0; st.liquid[ti] = 0; st.liquidType[ti] = 0;
178	      }
179	    }
180	    for (let j = py; j < n4; j++) {
181	      if (!st.inBounds(px, j)) continue;
182	      const ti = st.idx(px, j);
183	      st.type[ti] = SAND; st.flags[ti] = 1;
184	      st.liquid[ti] = 0; st.liquidType[ti] = 0; // ResetToType 清液体,墙保留
185	    }
186	  }
187	}
188	

```

21	    if (i === 1) left *= 2;
22	    let right = rng.int(35, 89);
23	    if (rng.next() * 3 < 1) right *= 2;
24	    if (i === 1) right *= 2;
25	    let x1 = x0 - left, x2 = x0 + right;
26	    if (x1 < 0) x1 = 0;
27	    if (x2 > w) x2 = w;
28	    if (i === 1) continue; // 中段跳过(原版 case 1: continue)
29	    if (i === 0) { x1 = 0; x2 = gs.beachLeftEnd; }
30	    if (i === 2) { x1 = gs.beachRightStart; x2 = w; }
31	    // 沙层厚度随机走(50-200)
32	    let depth = rng.int(50, 99);
33	    for (let x = x1; x < x2; x++) {
34	      if (rng.next() < 0.5) {
35	        depth += rng.int(-1, 1);
36	        if (depth < 50) depth = 50;
37	        if (depth > 200) depth = 200;

40	        const ii = st.idx(x, y);

42	        const edge = Math.min(depth, x - x1, x2 - x);
43	        const thick = edge + rng.int(0, 4);
44	        for (let y2 = y; y2 < y + thick && y2 < st.h; y2++) {
45	          const jj = st.idx(x, y2);
46	          if (x > x1 + rng.int(0, 4) && x < x2 - rng.int(0, 4)) {

50	        break;
51	      }
52	    }
53	  }
54	}

172	// ---- 原版 ShellPiles pass（WorldGen.cs:16385-16450 + ShellPile L10307-10420）----
173	// 每侧 50% 概率，最多 6 堆；ShellPile：沙面上椭圆随机漂移填 tile 495（贝壳堆块），

177	function shellPile(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, X: number, Y: number, worldSurface: number): boolean {
178	  if (X < 1 || Y < 1 || X >= st.w - 1 || Y >= st.h - 1) return false;
179	  let y = Y;
180	  while (y < st.h && !st.flags[st.idx(X, y)]) y++;
181	  if (y > worldSurface || y >= st.h) return false;          // 原版：仅地表内（海盆深度容差已放宽）
182	  if (st.type[st.idx(X, y)] !== T.SAND) return false;       // 必须沙面
183	  if (st.wall[st.idx(X, y)] !== 0) return false;            // 无背景墙
184	  y--;
185	  const SHELL = TILE_BY_KEY['v_495_shell_pile_block'] ?? 0;
186	  const HARD_SAND = TILE_BY_KEY['hardened_sand'] ?? 0;
187	  if (!SHELL) return false;
188	  let cx = X, cy = y;
189	  let vx = rng.next() * 0.6 - 0.3, vy = rng.next() * 0.5 + 0.5;
190	  let radius = rng.int(2, 4);
191	  if (rng.int(0, 9) === 0) radius += 1;
192	  let layers = rng.int(3, 6);
193	  const put = (x: number, yy: number, type: number) => {
194	    if (!st.inBounds(x, yy)) return;
195	    const i = st.idx(x, yy);
196	    st.type[i] = type; st.flags[i] = 1; st.frameX[i] = 0; st.frameY[i] = 0;
197	  };
198	  while (layers-- > 0) {
199	    const r = radius * (0.7 + rng.next() * 0.2);
200	    for (let x = Math.floor(X - radius * 4); x <= X + radius * 4; x++) {
201	      for (let yy = y + Math.floor(radius * 4); yy > y - radius * 4; yy--) {
202	        const dx = (cx - x) * 0.6, dyy = cy - yy;
203	        if (Math.hypot(dx, dyy) >= r) continue;
204	        if (yy <= y + 1 || rng.int(0, 5) === 0) {
205	          put(x, yy, SHELL);
206	          // 下方悬空补壳/沙/硬化沙（原版 10373-10399 三层下探）
207	          if (st.inBounds(x, yy + 1) && !st.flags[st.idx(x, yy + 1)]) {
208	            put(x, yy + 1, SHELL);
209	            if (st.inBounds(x, yy + 2) && !st.flags[st.idx(x, yy + 2)]) {
210	              put(x, yy + 2, T.SAND);
211	              if (st.inBounds(x, yy + 3) && !st.flags[st.idx(x, yy + 3)]) put(x, yy + 3, HARD_SAND);
212	            }
213	          }
214	        } else if (st.type[st.idx(x, yy)] !== SHELL) {
215	          put(x, yy, T.SAND);
216	        }
217	      }
218	    }
219	    cx += vx; cy += vy;
220	    vx = Math.max(-0.3, Math.min(0.3, vx + (rng.next() * 0.2 - 0.1)));
221	    vy = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(1.0, vy + (rng.next() * 0.2 - 0.1)));
222	  }
223	  return true;
224	}
225	
226	/** 海滩装饰：贝壳堆 + 海藻（GrowCheckSeaweed 简化 1:1：沙面+上方 4 格全满水才长） */
227	export function runBeachDecorPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
228	  const SEAWEED = TILE_BY_KEY['v_549_seaweed'] ?? 0;
229	  const sides: Array<[number, number, number]> = [
230	    [1, Math.max(1, gs.beachLeftEnd - 120), gs.beachLeftEnd],
231	    [-1, gs.beachRightStart, Math.min(st.w - 2, gs.beachRightStart + 120)],
232	  ];
233	  for (const [dir, x0, x1] of sides) {
234	    // 贝壳堆：50% 概率，锚点 = 海滩边界附近沙面（原版 shellStart 扫描近似）
235	    if (rng.next() < 0.5) {
236	      const baseX = dir === 1 ? gs.beachLeftEnd - rng.int(0, 5) : gs.beachRightStart + rng.int(0, 5);
237	      // 全列扫沙面（真实海盆深浅不一，固定窗口会漏锚）
238	      let by = -1;
239	      for (let y = 2; y < gs.worldSurface + 120 && y < st.h - 4; y++) {
240	        if (st.flags[st.idx(baseX, y)] && st.type[st.idx(baseX, y)] === T.SAND) { by = y; break; }
241	      }
242	      if (by > 0) {
243	        const offsets = [0, 10, 40, 70, 100, 25];
244	        for (const off of offsets) {
245	          if (off !== 0 && rng.next() >= 0.5) continue; // 原版 Next(maxValue 2..3) 门
246	          const x = dir === 1 ? baseX - off : baseX + off;
247	          shellPile(st, rng, x, Math.max(2, by - 50), gs.worldSurface + 120);
248	        }
249	      }
250	    }
251	    // 海藻：每个海洋列找沙面，上方 4 格全满水 → 2/3 放（原版 GrowCheckSeaweed 深水条件）
252	    if (SEAWEED) {
253	      for (let x = x0; x < x1; x++) {
254	        for (let y = 2; y < st.h - 4; y++) {
255	          const i = st.idx(x, y);
256	          if (!st.flags[i] || st.type[i] !== T.SAND) continue;
257	          const deep = [1, 2, 3, 4].every((d) => st.liquid[st.idx(x, y - d)] === 255 && !st.flags[st.idx(x, y - d)]);
258	          if (deep && rng.int(0, 2) !== 0) {
259	            const ai = st.idx(x, y - 1);
260	            st.type[ai] = SEAWEED; st.flags[ai] = 1; st.frameX[ai] = Math.floor(rng.next() * 3) * 18; st.frameY[ai] = 0;
261	          }
262	          break; // 每列只处理首个沙面
263	        }
264	      }
265	    }
266	  }
267	}
268

using System;
using Terraria.ID;

namespace Terraria.GameContent.Biomes.Desert;

public class SurfaceMap
{
	public readonly double Average;

	public readonly int Bottom;

	public readonly int Top;

	public readonly int X;

	private readonly short[] _heights;

	public int Width => _heights.Length;

	public short this[int absoluteX] => _heights[absoluteX - X];

	private SurfaceMap(short[] heights, int x)
	{
		_heights = heights;
		X = x;
		int num = 0;
		int num2 = int.MaxValue;
		int num3 = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < heights.Length; i++)
		{
			num3 += heights[i];
			num = Math.Max(num, heights[i]);
			num2 = Math.Min(num2, heights[i]);
		}
		if ((double)num > Main.worldSurface - 10.0)
		{
			num = (int)Main.worldSurface - 10;
		}
		Bottom = num;
		Top = num2;
		Average = (double)num3 / (double)_heights.Length;
	}

	public static SurfaceMap FromArea(int startX, int width)
	{
		int num = Main.maxTilesY / 2;
		short[] array = new short[width];
		for (int i = startX; i < startX + width; i++)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			int num2 = 0;
			for (int j = 50; j < 50 + num; j++)
			{
				if (Main.tile[i, j].active())
				{
					if (TileID.Sets.Clouds[Main.tile[i, j].type])
					{
						flag = false;
					}
					else if (!flag)
					{
						num2 = j;
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					num2 = num + 50;
				}
			}
			array[i - startX] = (short)num2;
		}
		return new SurfaceMap(array, startX);
	}
}

```

Interesting! SurfaceMap.FromArea — note the difference: the original sets `num2 = num + 50` INSIDE the loop for each j where !flag, meaning at the END num2 is num+50 unless flag is true. Our TS version computes `out[i] = found ? y : limit + 50;` — equivalent to checking flag at end. Wait, but there's a subtle difference: in C#, `num2 = num + 50` is assigned on every iteration where `!flag`. If flag becomes true at some point, then subsequent iterations don't reset. So final num2 = last solid non-cloud y if flag, else num+50. That matches our TS. OK.

But wait — there's a subtle bug difference: in C#, `if (!flag) num2 = num + 50;` runs after the active() check. If tile is active, non-cloud, and !flag → num2=j, flag=true. Then `if(!flag)` false. OK. Our TS: if found → y=j, found=true. Same. But: in C#, if tile active and cloud → flag=false, then `if (!flag) num2 = num+50` → num2 reset. In our TS, cloud case: `found = false; continue;` — continue skips the rest, equivalent since after the loop iteration nothing else happens. Actually in TS the `if (!found) { y = j; found = true; }` is skipped by continue. Equivalent. And final value same. Good.

Now find DunesBiome.cs — it's in Terraria.GameContent.Biomes.Dunes probably.

{
	private class DunesDescription
	{

		public Rectangle Area { get; private set; }

		public WindDirection WindDirection { get; private set; }

		private DunesDescription()
		{
		}

		public static DunesDescription CreateFromPlacement(Point origin, int width, int height)
		{
			Rectangle area = new Rectangle(origin.X - width / 2, origin.Y - height / 2, width, height);
			return new DunesDescription
			{
				Area = area,
				IsValid = true,
				Surface = SurfaceMap.FromArea(area.Left - 20, area.Width + 40),
				WindDirection = ((WorldGen.genRand.Next(2) != 0) ? WindDirection.Right : WindDirection.Left)
			};
		}
	}

	private enum WindDirection
	{
		Left,
		Right
	}

	public int MaximumWidth => _singleDunesWidth.ScaledMaximum * 2;

	public override bool Place(Point origin, StructureMap structures, GenerationProgress progress)
	{
		int height = (int)((double)GenBase._random.Next(60, 100) * _heightScale);
		int height2 = (int)((double)GenBase._random.Next(60, 100) * _heightScale);
		int random = _singleDunesWidth.GetRandom(GenBase._random);
		int random2 = _singleDunesWidth.GetRandom(GenBase._random);
		DunesDescription description = DunesDescription.CreateFromPlacement(new Point(origin.X - random / 2 + 30, origin.Y), random, height);
		DunesDescription description2 = DunesDescription.CreateFromPlacement(new Point(origin.X + random2 / 2 - 30, origin.Y), random2, height2);
		PlaceSingle(description, structures);
		PlaceSingle(description2, structures);
		return true;
	}

	private void PlaceSingle(DunesDescription description, StructureMap structures)
	{
		int num = GenBase._random.Next(3) + 8;
		for (int i = 0; i < num - 1; i++)
		{
			int num2 = (int)(2.0 / (double)num * (double)description.Area.Width);
			int num3 = (int)((double)i / (double)num * (double)description.Area.Width + (double)description.Area.Left) + num2 * 2 / 5;
			num3 += GenBase._random.Next(-5, 6);
			double num4 = (double)i / (double)(num - 2);
			double num5 = 1.0 - Math.Abs(num4 - 0.5) * 2.0;
			PlaceHill(num3 - num2 / 2, num3 + num2 / 2, (num5 * 0.3 + 0.2) * _heightScale, description);
		}
		int num6 = GenBase._random.Next(2) + 1;
		for (int j = 0; j < num6; j++)
		{
			int num7 = description.Area.Width / 2;
			int x = description.Area.Center.X;
			x += GenBase._random.Next(-10, 11);
			PlaceHill(x - num7 / 2, x + num7 / 2, 0.8 * _heightScale, description);
		}
		structures.AddStructure(description.Area, 20);
	}

	private static void PlaceHill(int startX, int endX, double scale, DunesDescription description)
	{
		Point startPoint = new Point(startX, description.Surface[startX]);
		Point endPoint = new Point(endX, description.Surface[endX]);
		Point point = new Point((startPoint.X + endPoint.X) / 2, (startPoint.Y + endPoint.Y) / 2 - (int)(35.0 * scale));
		int num = (endPoint.X - point.X) / 4;
		int minValue = (endPoint.X - point.X) / 16;
		if (description.WindDirection == WindDirection.Left)
		{
			point.X -= WorldGen.genRand.Next(minValue, num + 1);
		}
		else

		}

		Point point3 = new Point(point2.X / -2, point2.Y / -2);
		PlaceCurvedLine(startPoint, point, (description.WindDirection != WindDirection.Left) ? point3 : point2, description);
		PlaceCurvedLine(point, endPoint, (description.WindDirection == WindDirection.Left) ? point3 : point2, description);

using Newtonsoft.Json;
using Newtonsoft.Json.Converters;
using Terraria.Utilities;

namespace Terraria.WorldBuilding;

public class WorldGenRange
{
	public enum ScalingMode
	{
		None,
		WorldArea,
		WorldWidth
	}

	public static readonly WorldGenRange Empty = new WorldGenRange(0, 0);

	[JsonProperty("Min")]
	public readonly int Minimum;

	[JsonProperty("Max")]
	public readonly int Maximum;

	[JsonProperty]
	[JsonConverter(typeof(StringEnumConverter))]
	public readonly ScalingMode ScaleWith;

	public int ScaledMinimum => ScaleValue(Minimum);

	public int ScaledMaximum => ScaleValue(Maximum);

	public WorldGenRange(int minimum, int maximum)
	{
		Minimum = minimum;
		Maximum = maximum;
	}

	public int GetRandom(UnifiedRandom random)
	{
		return random.Next(ScaledMinimum, ScaledMaximum + 1);
	}

	private int ScaleValue(int value)
	{
		double num = 1.0;
		switch (ScaleWith)
		{
		case ScalingMode.WorldArea:
			num = (double)(Main.maxTilesX * Main.maxTilesY) / 5040000.0;
			break;
		case ScalingMode.WorldWidth:
			num = (double)Main.maxTilesX / 4200.0;
			break;
		case ScalingMode.None:
			num = 1.0;
			break;
		}
		return (int)(num * (double)value);
	}
}

```

// 确定性随机数：Terraria 1.4.5.6 位级对齐。
//
// ── UnifiedRandom（Terraria.Utilities/UnifiedRandom.cs 1:1）─────────────────
// .NET System.Random 的 Knuth 减法生成器（MSEED=161803398），纯 int32 运算。
// 位级移植要点（JS/C# 数值差异全部钉死，改动前先读）：
//  1. C# int 溢出 unchecked 回绕 ↔ JS：本实现所有中间值都落在 int32 范围内
//     （diff 最小 -(2^31-1)，`+((n>>31)&0x7FFFFFFF)` 归位后 ∈[0,2^31-1]），
//     无需模拟回绕；存储用 Int32Array 与 C# int[] 同构。
//  2. `(int)double` 是向零截断 ↔ JS Math.trunc（Sample()≥0 时与 floor 等价，
//     但必须写 trunc 以防有人把 internalSample 改出负值）。
//  3. `Sample()` 常数 4.656612875245797E-10 = 1/(2^31-1)，十进制字面量两语言
//     解析为同一 double，乘法 IEEE754 位级一致。
//  4. `Next(min,max)` 的 range 用 long（≤2^32）↔ JS number 精确整数，安全。
//     大 range 分支的 `(int)(long+min)` unchecked 回绕 ↔ JS `|0`（ToInt32 mod 2^32）。
//  5. `GetSampleForLargeRange` 的 `% 2 == 0`：C# 余数带符号但只判零，JS `%` 同判。
//  金标：tests/golden/unified-random.json 由真·Terraria.exe 反射导出
//  （tools/golden/rand-golden.cs），tests/unified-random.test.ts 位级断言。
//
// ── 种子解析（WorldFileData.TranslateSeed, cs:399-410 1:1）────────────────
// 数字字符串 → Math.Abs(int)（int.MinValue→int.MaxValue）；非数字 →
// ReLogic.Utilities.Crc32.Calculate（IEEE 表，按 UTF-16 code unit 逐字符，
// 注意 `(crc ^ c) & 0xFF` 只取 char 低字节）。Crc32 结果可为负，直接作种子。
export class UnifiedRandom {
  private seedArray: Int32Array;
  private inext = 0;

  constructor(seed: number) {
    this.seedArray = new Int32Array(56);
    this.setSeed(seed);
  }

  /** C# SetSeed（UnifiedRandom.cs:25-61）1:1 */
  setSeed(seed: number): void {
    this.seedArray.fill(0);
    // C#: (Seed == int.MinValue) ? int.MaxValue : Math.Abs(Seed)
    const num = seed === -2147483648 ? 2147483647 : Math.abs(seed);
    let num2 = 161803398 - num;
    this.seedArray[55] = num2;
    let num3 = 1;
    for (let j = 1; j < 55; j++) {
      const num4 = (21 * j) % 55;
      this.seedArray[num4] = num3;
      num3 = (num2 - num3) | 0; // C# unchecked int：可能回绕，|0 = ToInt32 同语义
      if (num3 < 0) num3 += 2147483647; // C# += int.MaxValue（不是 2^32！）
      num2 = this.seedArray[num4];
    }
    for (let k = 1; k < 5; k++) {
      for (let l = 1; l < 56; l++) {
        this.seedArray[l] -= this.seedArray[1 + ((l + 30) % 55)];
        if (this.seedArray[l] < 0) this.seedArray[l] += 2147483647;
      }
    }
    this.inext = 0;
  }

  /** C# Sample（:63-65）*/
  private sample(): number {
    return this.internalSample() * 4.656612875245797e-10;
  }

  /** C# InternalSample（:67-86）1:1。inext 为 uint 语义（0..55，无溢出面）*/
  private internalSample(): number {
    let num = this.inext + 1;
    if (num > 55) num = 1;
    let num2 = num + 21;
    if (num2 > 55) num2 -= 55;
    let num3 = this.seedArray[num] - this.seedArray[num2];
    if (num3 === 2147483647) num3--;
    num3 = num3 + ((num3 >> 31) & 0x7fffffff); // >>31 算术移位，JS/C# 一致
    this.seedArray[num] = num3;
    this.inext = num;
    return num3;
  }

  /** C# GetSampleForLargeRange（:96-104）。注意两次 InternalSample 的求值顺序 */
  private getSampleForLargeRange(): number {
    let num = this.internalSample();
    if (this.internalSample() % 2 === 0) num = -num;
    return (num + 2147483646) / 4294967293;
  }

  /** C# Next()（:106-108）：裸 int32 */
  next(): number {
    return this.internalSample();
  }

  /** C# Next(minValue, maxValue)（:115-133）：[min, max)。maxValue 负值时 C# 抛异常，
   *  本移植不抛（生成期契约保证入参合法）。*/
  nextIntRange(minValue: number, maxValue: number): number {
    const num = maxValue - minValue; // C# long 减法，≤2^32，JS 精确
    if (num <= 2147483647) {
      return (Math.trunc(this.sample() * num) + minValue) | 0;
    }
    return (Math.trunc(this.getSampleForLargeRange() * num) + minValue) | 0;
  }

  /** C# Next(maxValue)（:121-127）：[0, max) */
  nextInt(maxValue: number): number {
    return Math.trunc(this.sample() * maxValue);
  }

  /** C# NextDouble()（:113-115）*/
  nextDouble(): number {
    return this.sample();
  }
}

// ── RNG：世界生成/运行时统一入口（API 不变，内核换 UnifiedRandom）──────────
// 与原版调用点的对账约定（gen 代码全部按此书写）：
//   rng.next()   = NextDouble()          每次消耗 1 个 InternalSample
//   rng.int(a,b) = Next(a, b+1)          闭区间 [a,b]
//   rng.chance(p)= NextDouble() < p      1 次
//   rng.pick(xs) = xs[Next(xs.length)]   1 次
//   rng.range(a,b) = a + NextDouble()*(b-a)
export class RNG {
  private ur: UnifiedRandom;
  constructor(seed: number) {
    this.ur = new UnifiedRandom(seed);
  }

```

using System;

namespace Terraria.Utilities;

[Serializable]
public class UnifiedRandom
{
	private const int MBIG = int.MaxValue;

	private const int MSEED = 161803398;

	private const int MZ = 0;

	private uint inext;

	private int[] SeedArray = new int[56];

	public UnifiedRandom()
		: this(Environment.TickCount)
	{
	}

	public UnifiedRandom(int Seed)
	{
		SetSeed(Seed);
	}

	public void SetSeed(int Seed)
	{
		for (int i = 0; i < SeedArray.Length; i++)
		{
			SeedArray[i] = 0;
		}
		int num = ((Seed == int.MinValue) ? int.MaxValue : Math.Abs(Seed));
		int num2 = 161803398 - num;
		SeedArray[55] = num2;
		int num3 = 1;
		for (int j = 1; j < 55; j++)
		{
			int num4 = 21 * j % 55;
			SeedArray[num4] = num3;
			num3 = num2 - num3;
			if (num3 < 0)
			{
				num3 += int.MaxValue;
			}
			num2 = SeedArray[num4];
		}
		for (int k = 1; k < 5; k++)
		{
			for (int l = 1; l < 56; l++)
			{
				SeedArray[l] -= SeedArray[1 + (l + 30) % 55];
				if (SeedArray[l] < 0)
				{
					SeedArray[l] += int.MaxValue;
				}
			}
		}
		inext = 0u;
	}

	protected double Sample()
	{
		return (double)InternalSample() * 4.656612875245797E-10;
	}

	private int InternalSample()
	{
		uint num = inext + 1;
		if (num > 55)
		{
			num = 1u;
		}
		uint num2 = num + 21;
		if (num2 > 55)
		{
			num2 -= 55;
		}
		int[] seedArray = SeedArray;
		int num3 = seedArray[num] - seedArray[num2];
		if (num3 == int.MaxValue)
		{
			num3--;
		}
		num3 = (seedArray[num] = num3 + ((num3 >> 31) & 0x7FFFFFFF));
		inext = num;
		return num3;
	}

	public int Peek()
	{
		uint num = inext + 1;
		if (num > 55)
		{
			num = 1u;
		}
		uint num2 = num + 21;
		if (num2 > 55)
		{
			num2 -= 55;
		}
		return SeedArray[num] - SeedArray[num2];
	}

	public int Next()
	{
		return InternalSample();
	}

	private double GetSampleForLargeRange()
	{
		int num = InternalSample();
		if (InternalSample() % 2 == 0)
		{
			num = -num;
		}
		return ((double)num + 2147483646.0) / 4294967293.0;
	}

	public int Next(int minValue, int maxValue)
	{
		if (minValue > maxValue)
		{
			throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("minValue", "minValue must be less than maxValue");
		}
		long num = (long)maxValue - (long)minValue;
		if (num <= int.MaxValue)
		{
			return (int)(Sample() * (double)num) + minValue;
		}
		return (int)((long)(GetSampleForLargeRange() * (double)num) + minValue);
	}

	public int Next(int maxValue)
	{
		if (maxValue < 0)
		{
			throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("maxValue", "maxValue must be positive.");
		}

  next(): number {
    return this.ur.nextDouble();
  }

  /** 闭区间 [min, max]，≡ 原版 Next(min, max+1) */
  int(min: number, max: number): number {
    return this.ur.nextIntRange(min, max + 1);
  }

  range(min: number, max: number): number {
    return min + this.ur.nextDouble() * (max - min);
  }

  chance(p: number): boolean {
    return this.ur.nextDouble() < p;
  }

  pick<T>(arr: T[]): T {
    return arr[this.ur.nextInt(arr.length)];
  }

  /** 原版裸方法直通（Reset 序列/pass 级对账用，语义与 C# 完全一致） */
  nextInt(maxValue: number): number {
    return this.ur.nextInt(maxValue);
  }

  nextIntRange(minValue: number, maxValue: number): number {
    return this.ur.nextIntRange(minValue, maxValue);
  }

  get vanilla(): UnifiedRandom {
    return this.ur;
  }
}

// ── Crc32（ReLogic.Utilities.Crc32 反编译 1:1）─────────────────────────────
// 标准 IEEE 表（多项式 0xEDB88320）。按 UTF-16 code unit 逐字符：
// (num ^ c) & 0xFF 只取 charCode 低字节——charCode > 0xFF 时高字节不参与。
const CRC32_TAB = (() => {
  const t = new Uint32Array(256);
  for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) {
    let c = i;
    for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) c = c & 1 ? 0xedb88320 ^ (c >>> 1) : c >>> 1;
    t[i] = c >>> 0;
  }
  return t;
})();

export function crc32Calculate(value: string): number {
  let num = 0xffffffff;
  for (let i = 0; i < value.length; i++) {
    num = (CRC32_TAB[(num ^ value.charCodeAt(i)) & 0xff] ^ (num >>> 8)) >>> 0;
  }
  return ~num | 0; // C# (int)(~uint)：位翻转后按 int32 解释（可为负）
}

// ── 种子解析（WorldFileData.TranslateSeed, cs:399-410 1:1）─────────────────
// 数字字符串 → Abs(int)（int.MinValue 特判）；否则 Crc32 直出。
// C# int.TryParse 细节：前后空白/正负号/前导零合法，溢出或空串 → false 走 Crc32。
// （Unicode 数字字符 C# 能解析而本实现不认——种子 UI 只产 ASCII，不构成偏差。）
export function translateSeed(seedText: string): number {
  const m = /^\s*([+-]?\d+)\s*$/.exec(seedText);
  if (m) {
    const v = BigInt(m[1]);
    if (v >= -2147483648n && v <= 2147483647n) {
      const n = Number(v);
      return n === -2147483648 ? 2147483647 : Math.abs(n);
    }
  }
  return crc32Calculate(seedText);
}

// FNV-1a 字符串 hash（旧种子派生/通用标识用；世界生成种子已改 translateSeed）
export function hashString(str: string): number {
  let h = 2166136261 >>> 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
    h ^= str.charCodeAt(i);
    h = Math.imul(h, 16777619);
  }
  return h >>> 0;
}

// 2D 整数 hash → [0,1)，无状态确定性噪声（渲染层视觉变体用，与种子等价无关）
export function hash2(x: number, y: number, seed: number): number {
  let h = seed >>> 0;
  h = Math.imul(h ^ (x | 0), 0x27d4eb2d);
  h = Math.imul(h ^ (y | 0), 0x85ebca6b);
  h ^= h >>> 15;
  h = Math.imul(h, 0x2c1b3c6d);
  h ^= h >>> 12;
  return (h >>> 0) / 4294967296;
}

Our: `sandDepthLimit = Math.floor((gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2)` then `for y < sandDepthLimit`. C#: k < (ws+rl)/2.0 — k integer, compare with double. Number of iterations = ceil((ws+rl)/2) if not integer... Let's be precise: iterations count = smallest integer k_max such that k_max >= (ws+rl)/2 → k ranges 0..ceil(v) where v=(ws+rl)/2. Count = ceil(v) (if v not integer) or v (if integer). Our Math.floor(v) → count = floor(v). DIFFERENT when v is not an integer: e.g. ws=100, rl=130 → v=115.0 fine. But ws+rl odd → v = x.5 → C# count = ceil = floor+1. Our count = floor. Hmm! worldSurface and rockLayer are doubles in C# (Main.worldSurface is double). So v is generally non-integer. C# k iterates while k < v, so k_max = ceil(v) iterations (k from 0 to ceil(v)-1). Our floor → potentially 1 fewer iteration. Impact: the inner scan for first active tile might miss a tile that exists between floor and ceil. Rare, minor-medium. Actually more importantly the depth of scan differs by at most 1 row → 无害到中等. Worth flagging.

Also `gs.worldSurface`/`gs.rockLevel` — need to check GenState types (probably ints). In C# Main.worldSurface/rockLayer are doubles. Our gs values likely ints. That's fine.

11. `num4`/`num5` boundaries and the k-loop's `break` — ✓ break present in both.

12. Our TS also has `y2 < st.h` guard in the l-loop: C# has no such guard (`for (int l = k; l < k + num7; l++)`), would index out of range... but ocean tiles within world. Harmless.

13. Our TS writes `st.type[jj] = T.SAND` without setting active flag — C# `Main.tile[j, l].type = 53;` — also only sets type, keeps active (tile already active since we found active at k). ✓ matches. But our TS also doesn't clear liquid — same as C#. ✓.

14. `if (x > x1 + rng.int(0,4) && x < x2 - rng.int(0,4))` ✓ matches C# `j > num4 + genRand.Next(5) && j < num5 - genRand.Next(5)`.

Wait, C# Next(5) returns [0,5). Our rng.int(0,4) → Next(0,5) → [0,5). ✓.

15. RNG consumption `Next(2)` per column: C# `if (genRand.Next(2) == 0)` then `num6 += genRand.Next(-1, 2)`. Our: `if (rng.next() < 0.5) { depth += rng.int(-1,1); ... }`. rng.next() < 0.5 ⟺ NextDouble() < 0.5 ⟺ (int)(Sample*2)==0 ⟺ Next(2)==0 ✓. And rng.int(-1,1) = Next(-1,2) ✓.

16. Note the l-loop in C# consumes Next(5) up to 2× per l iteration EVEN IF the condition results in no tile write — and our TS matches (consumes regardless of inBounds? Our TS line 44 loop: `for (let y2 = y; y2 < y + thick && y2 < st.h; y2++)`. The `y2 < st.h` guard could TRUNCATE the loop early and thus SKIP RNG consumptions for l >= st.h! C# has no such bound → consumes num7 times always. Difference only if k + num7 > maxTilesY — unlikely in ocean (ocean surface near top). 无害, but note.

Now the pyramid check placement — verify our TS truly lacks it. Lines 39-51 — yes, no midpoint check, no Next(6). Also, does GenState have pyramidSpots consumed by StructuresPass? The DunesPass pushes candidates. OceanSand also should. Let me grep for pyramidSpots usage.

Also check: C# `GenVars.PyrX` array sized random+3 where random = count of dunes; numPyr may exceed capacity? Original quirk. Our pyramidSpots is a growing array. Not RNG.

Also I should verify the missing `SetupDungeonGenVarVariables` RNG consumption. Let me check what it does.

Also check GenBase._random assignment.

Also check whether our runDunesPass caller handles SetupDungeonGenVarVariables elsewhere (DungeonPass). Let me grep in game dir for SetupDungeon / dungeonGenVars.

Also need to check Tile.ClearEverything / ResetToType for wall semantics.

// 原版 pass "Dunes"(cs:11540):沙丘塑造 + 金字塔候选(gs.pyramidSpots)
function vanillaDunes({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {
  const gs = ctxGs[0];
  if (!gs) return;
  runDunesPass(store, rng, gs);
}

// 原版 pass "Shimmer"(cs:15256):以太微光生物群系
function vanillaShimmer({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {
  const gs = ctxGs[0];
  if (!gs) return;
  runShimmerPass(store, rng, gs);
}

// 原版 pass "Micro Biomes"(cs:21785):露营地标 + 炸药陷阱屋
function vanillaMicroBiomes({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {
  const gs = ctxGs[0];
  if (!gs) return;
  runMicroBiomesPass(store, rng, gs);
}

function vanillaLakes({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {
  // 原版 pass 31 Lakes + pass 36 Gems（cs 注册序 31<36）
  const gs = ctxGs[0];
  if (!gs) return;
  runLakesPass(store, rng, gs);
  runGemsPass(store, rng, gs);
}

function vanillaShinies({ store, rng }: GenCtx) {
  // 原版 pass 27 Shinies（替换自研 orePass）
  const gs = ctxGs[0];

// 原版 pass 15-22:冰原→草种→丛林→泥转丛林草→沙漠→蘑菇→大理石/花岗岩
function vanillaBiomes({ store, rng, world }: GenCtx) {
  const gs = ctxGs[0];
  if (!gs) return;
  runIceBiomePass(store, rng, gs);       // 15 Generate Ice Biome
  runGrassPass(store, rng, gs);          // 16 Grass
  const jx = runJunglePass(store, rng, gs); // JunglePass
  world.jungleX = jx;
  // 丛林表面泥化:泥柱核心区 ±0.12w 内,表层 10 格 dirt→mud(原版巨型 runner
  // 覆盖表面所致;我们补这一步让丛林表面成草皮而非裸土)
  {
    const core = (gs as any).jungleCoreX ?? jx;
    const reach = Math.floor(store.w * 0.12);
    for (let x = Math.max(0, core - reach); x < Math.min(store.w, core + reach); x++) {
      let sy = 0;
      while (sy < store.h - 12 && !store.flags[store.idx(x, sy)]) sy++;
      for (let y = sy; y < sy + 10; y++) {
        if (store.get(x, y) === T.DIRT) store.setTileSilent(x, y, TILE_BY_KEY['mud']!);
      }
    }
  }
  spreadGrassAll(store, TILE_BY_KEY['mud']!, TILE_BY_KEY['v_60_jungle_grass_block']!, world.jungleX, Math.floor(store.w * 0.21)); // 17 Mud Caves To Grass(丛林核心区)
  runDesertPass(store, rng, gs);         // 18 Full Desert
  runFloatingIslandsPass(store, rng, gs); // 19 Floating Islands
  runMushroomPass(store, rng, gs);       // 20 Mushroom Patches
  runMarbleGranitePass(store, rng, gs);  // 21/22 Marble/Granite
  runDirtToMudAndSiltPass(store, rng, gs); // 23 Dirt To Mud / 24 Silt
  runSlushPass(store, gs);                 // 原版 pass 33 Slush（在 DirtToMud/Silt(25/26) 之后——泥已生成）
  runCorruptionPass(store, rng, gs);     // 28 Corruption/Crimson

  world.dungeonX = gs.dungeonEntX || gs.dungeonLocation;
  world.dungeonY = gs.dungeonEntY || gs.dungeonY || 0;

  gs.snowOriginRight = Math.min(W, dX + snowR);

// 原版生成管线共享状态（对应 WorldGen.GenerateWorld 闭包变量 L4694-4741 + Reset pass 掷的骰子）。
// 105 个 pass 按序读写,顺序不可调换。
export interface GenState {
  // 世界常量（Reset / Terrain pass 输出）
  worldSurface: number;   // 平均地表 y（tile）
  worldSurfaceLow: number;
  worldSurfaceHigh: number;
  rockLevel: number;
  rockLevelLow: number;
  rockLevelHigh: number;
  waterLine: number;      // 雪原上方（原版 waterLine ≈ 0.35×h）
  lavaLine: number;       // 地狱顶（原版 ≈ h-200）
  beachDistance: number;
  /** GenVars.worldSurface/rockLayer：Terrain 游走**终值**（double，≠ Main.worldSurface
   *  的 worldSurfaceHigh+25；SandPatches/SmallHoles/Tunnels 等按 GenVars 值掷骰） */
  genWorldSurface: number;
  genRockLayer: number;
  /** Tunnels pass 记录（cs:11743：numTunnels ≤ maxTunnels-1；tunnelX[5] 存档用） */
  tunnelX: number[];
  numTunnels: number;

  // Reset pass 掷的世界级选择
  crimson: boolean;           // 50% 猩红 / 50% 腐化
  oreTiers: { copper: number; iron: number; silver: number; gold: number }; // 含替代 id
  dungeonSide: 1 | -1;        // 地牢在地图哪侧
  dungeonX: number;           // 地牢位置（Reset num4：仅作雪原中心）
  dungeonLocation: number;    // 地牢入口实际位置（Reset 末掷）
  dungeonY: number;           // 地牢入口地表 Y（DungeonPass 回填；刷怪门槛 dungeonY+40 用）
  dungeonEntX: number;        // 入口门厅可站立点 X（DungeonPass 回填；原版 dungeonX 语义=老头锚）
  dungeonEntY: number;        // 入口门厅可站立点 Y
  jungleX: number;            // 丛林（与地牢异侧）
  snowOriginLeft: number;
  snowOriginRight: number;
  beachLeftEnd: number;       // 左海滩右界（Reset L4880 掷，Ocean Sand/Beaches 消费）
  beachRightStart: number;    // 右海滩左界

  // 各 pass 记录给后续 pass 用的区域
  // RandomizeTreeStyle（Reset 掷）：树冠样式边界与样式（finalize 写回 world）
  treeX: number[];
  treeStyle: number[];
  /** 生成的树登记（{x, y=树顶, h}；finalize 写 world.trees 供砍伐整棵判定） */
  genTrees: Array<{ x: number; y: number; h: number }>;
  /** Lakes pass 记录的湖位 x（后续 pass 避让用） */
  lakeX: number[];
  // Mount Caves（pass 7）记录的山丘坐标（pass 34/Webs 消费）
  mCaveX: Int32Array;
  mCaveY: Int32Array;
  numMCaves: number;
  snowMinX: Int32Array | null; // 每行雪原左边界（C# int[]；Ice Biome pass 写）
  snowMaxX: Int32Array | null;
  snowTop: number; snowBottom: number;
  jungleBounds: [number, number] | null; // [minX, maxX]
  evilZones: Array<{ x0: number; x1: number }>;
  undergroundDesert: { x0: number; y0: number; x1: number; y1: number } | null;

  // 结构候选（由早期 pass 排队、后期 pass 消费）
  pyramidSpots: Array<{ x: number; y: number }>;
  floatingIslands: Array<{ x: number; y: number; style: number; isLake: boolean }>;
  /** 微光以太中心（Shimmer pass cs:15301 GenVars.shimmerPosition；-1 = 未生成） */
  shimmerX: number;
  shimmerY: number;

  // 宝箱战利品序（AddBuriedChest 全局态,cs:11262/36165/10130）
  hellChestItems: number[] | null; // 每世界乱序 [274,220,112,218,3019]（首只地狱箱时掷）
  hellChestIdx: number;            // 地狱箱主件序游标（成功一只 +1,循环）
  jungleItemIdx: number;           // 常春藤箱主件序游标（JungleItemCount）
  gennedMahoganyWands: boolean;    // 活桃花心木法杖对已给过标记（GenVars.gennedLivingMahoganyWands）
}

export function newGenState(w: number, h: number): GenState {
  return {
    worldSurface: Math.floor(h * 0.3), worldSurfaceLow: 0, worldSurfaceHigh: 0,
    rockLevel: Math.floor(h * 0.42), rockLevelLow: 0, rockLevelHigh: 0,
    waterLine: Math.floor(h * 0.35), lavaLine: h - 200,
    beachDistance: Math.max(24, Math.floor(w * 0.06)),
    genWorldSurface: 0, genRockLayer: 0,
    tunnelX: new Array(50).fill(0), numTunnels: 0,
    crimson: false,
    oreTiers: { copper: 0, iron: 0, silver: 0, gold: 0 },
    dungeonSide: 1, dungeonX: 0, dungeonLocation: 0, dungeonY: 0, dungeonEntX: 0, dungeonEntY: 0, jungleX: 0,
    snowOriginLeft: 0, snowOriginRight: 0,
    beachLeftEnd: 0, beachRightStart: 0,
    treeX: [0, 0, 0], treeStyle: [0, 0, 0, 0], genTrees: [], lakeX: [],
    mCaveX: new Int32Array(1024), mCaveY: new Int32Array(1024), numMCaves: 0,
    snowMinX: null, snowMaxX: null, snowTop: 0, snowBottom: 0,
    jungleBounds: null,
    evilZones: [],
    undergroundDesert: null,
    pyramidSpots: [],
    floatingIslands: [],
    shimmerX: -1, shimmerY: -1,
    hellChestItems: null, hellChestIdx: 0, jungleItemIdx: 0, gennedMahoganyWands: false,
  };
}

```

## 结论

- **Ocean Sand**：结构移植质量高（拒绝采样、双 35-90 段、条件掷骰、短路顺序、`(int)` 截断全部正确），但存在 **1 处致命缺失**：cs:11682 的中点金字塔 `Next(6)` 条件掷骰（连带候选丢失），每世界少掷 2 颗。
- **Dunes**：单看 `runDunesPass`/`placeDunes`/`placeSingle`/`placeHill`/`placeCurvedLine` 的 RNG 消耗序列与原版**逐颗位级一致**（这是两个 pass 里做得最扎实的部分）；致命问题在 pass 边界——**cs:11542 的地牢主题 `Next(3)` 未在本 pass 前消耗**（被错放到 DungeonPass），另 WorldGen.ts:282「SetupDungeonGenVars 零掷骰」的注释结论需要一并纠正。
- 修复优先级：① 补 OceanSand 中点 `Next(6)`（BeachPass.ts:39 内、首个实心格处）；② 把地牢主题 `Next(3)` 移回 Dunes pass 前（或在 `runDunesPass` 开头消耗等价值）。两处修掉后，这两个 pass 的 RNG 流即可与原版对齐；D2（StructureMap）/D3（清墙）是第二梯队，D4/D5/D6/D7/D8 及 B2/B3/B4 可不动。
```
